# Lleida — candidates

- Target CSV: `data/csv/es/catalunya/lleida.csv`
- Discovery source: the directory record linked in the candidate row, followed to the current producer domain where available.
- Review date: 2026-08-26.
- Scope: 5 unresolved holds after current-source admission review.
- Cutoff: public pages reviewed on 2026-08-26; later changes and private records are out of scope.
- Remaining work: recheck a current first-party source that proves active own production, public offer, and productive municipality before materialization.

## DOP Les Garrigues registered-cooperative sweep — 2026-09-03

- Discovery source: <https://olidoplesgarrigues.com/cooperatives-inscrites/>, the registered cooperative and mill list published by the Consell Regulador DOP Les Garrigues.
- Search date: 2026-09-03.
- Scope: all 20 entities published by the council, de-duplicated against every `Aceite` row in the denomination municipalities by name and municipality. Ten were already represented; the 10 below are open Level 1 leads.
- Cutoff: the council list as published on 2026-09-03. The council publishes names without addresses, so the municipality below is read from the entity's own name where it carries one.
- Source ceiling: the council register supports membership, product and the entity identity it publishes. It does not by itself establish current activity, a public own offer, online sales or the productive municipality.

## RGSEAA coffee, cocoa and infusion elaborators — 2026-09-03

- Discovery source: the [RGSEAA public search](https://rgsa-web-aesan.mscbs.es/rgsa/formulario_principal_js.jsp), key 25 "Alimentos estimulantes, especies vegetales para infusiones y sus derivados", category 1 "Fabricación o Elaboración o Transformación".
- Search date: 2026-09-03.
- Scope: all 857 establishments the register returns for that key and category across Spain, reduced to those whose registered name publicly signals a coffee, cocoa or infusion producer identity, then de-duplicated against the current area CSVs and this note by name and municipality. 1 open Level 1 lead falls in this area.
- Cutoff: the register as queried on 2026-09-03. No first-party site, brand, denomination council or shop was consulted in this pass.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. The register supports the legal entity, its registered activity class and the industrial address it publishes, and its `localidad` may be a pedanía rather than the official municipality.

| name | municipality / geographic clue | probable category / output | reason it may qualify | discovery | official | remaining work |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CAFES ROMA SCP | Balaguer | `Café` | RGSEAA registers it under key 25 for manufacturing or elaboration, entry 25.00837/L | [RGSEAA search](https://rgsa-web-aesan.mscbs.es/rgsa/formulario_principal_js.jsp) |  | Confirm an own roastery rather than repackaging third-party coffee, and a current offer under this identity. Resolve the official municipality from the registered address "CR Comte D'Urgell 16". |

## RIAAC olive-mill, apiary and cheese elaborators — 2026-09-03

- Discovery source: the [Indústries agroalimentàries de Catalunya](https://analisi.transparenciacatalunya.cat/Medi-Rural-Pesca/Ind-stries-agroaliment-ries-de-Catalunya/p28j-xe65) open dataset, which publishes the Registre d'Indústries Agràries i Alimentàries de Catalunya (RIAAC).
- Search date: 2026-09-03.
- Scope: the four RIAAC main-activity classes that map to olive oil, honey and cheese — *Obtenció d'oli d'oliva verge (almàssera)*, *Extracció i preparació de mel i altres productes apícoles*, *Elaboració de formatges frescos* and *Elaboració de formatges madurats* — which return 831 rows for 471 distinct registrations. De-duplicated by registration number, then against the area CSVs by name and municipality. 47 open Level 1 leads fall in this area.
- Cutoff: the dataset as queried on 2026-09-03. Only these four classes were read; the register's remaining activity classes, wine above all, are a later batch.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. RIAAC supports the registered establishment, its activity class and the industrial address it publishes, never current activity, a public own offer or online sales. Its `municipi` cell carries the locality after the municipality, and many registrations are held by a natural person rather than a trading identity.

## RIAAC olive-mill, apiary and cheese elaborators — identity reconciliation — 2026-09-03

- Discovery source: the [Indústries agroalimentàries de Catalunya](https://analisi.transparenciacatalunya.cat/Medi-Rural-Pesca/Ind-stries-agroaliment-ries-de-Catalunya/p28j-xe65) open dataset, which publishes the Registre d'Indústries Agràries i Alimentàries de Catalunya (RIAAC).
- Search date: 2026-09-03.
- Scope: the four RIAAC main-activity classes that map to olive oil, honey and cheese — *Obtenció d'oli d'oliva verge (almàssera)*, *Extracció i preparació de mel i altres productes apícoles*, *Elaboració de formatges frescos* and *Elaboració de formatges madurats* — which return 831 rows for 471 distinct registrations. De-duplicated by registration number, then against the area CSVs by name and municipality. 26 further registrations in this area already ha a published row of the same category in the same municipality, so each is held on one question: is it that row under its trading name, or a different productive unit?
- Cutoff: the dataset as queried on 2026-09-03. Only these four classes were read; the register's remaining activity classes, wine above all, are a later batch.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. RIAAC supports the registered establishment, its activity class and the industrial address it publishes, never current activity, a public own offer or online sales. Its `municipi` cell carries the locality after the municipality, and many registrations are held by a natural person rather than a trading identity.

## RIAAC wine and vermouth elaborators — identity reconciliation — 2026-09-03

- Discovery source: the [Indústries agroalimentàries de Catalunya](https://analisi.transparenciacatalunya.cat/Medi-Rural-Pesca/Ind-stries-agroaliment-ries-de-Catalunya/p28j-xe65) open dataset, which publishes the Registre d'Indústries Agràries i Alimentàries de Catalunya (RIAAC).
- Search date: 2026-09-03.
- Scope: the six RIAAC main-activity classes that elaborate wine — *amb DOP/IGP*, *sense DOP/IGP*, *escumós* in bottle and in tank, *vi de licor* and *begudes aromatitzades a base de vi* — which return 3,759 rows for 971 distinct registrations. De-duplicated by registration number, then against the area CSVs by name and municipality. Bottling, ageing and enological storage classes are excluded as non-elaboration. 13 further registrations in this area sit in a municipality that has only one or two published wine rows, so each is held on one question: is it one of those rows under its trading name, or a different winery? Registrations in municipalities with three or more published wine rows are listed as leads instead, because co-presence proves nothing there.
- Cutoff: the dataset as queried on 2026-09-03. No denomination council, first-party site or brand was consulted in this pass.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. RIAAC supports the registered establishment, its activity class and the industrial address it publishes, never current activity, a public own offer or online sales. Its `municipi` cell carries the locality after the municipality, and many registrations are held by a natural person rather than a trading identity.

## RIAAC meat, bakery, preserve and other elaborators — 2026-09-04

- Discovery source: the [Indústries agroalimentàries de Catalunya](https://analisi.transparenciacatalunya.cat/Medi-Rural-Pesca/Ind-stries-agroaliment-ries-de-Catalunya/p28j-xe65) open dataset, which publishes the Registre d'Indústries Agràries i Alimentàries de Catalunya (RIAAC).
- Search date: 2026-09-04.
- Scope: the 69 remaining RIAAC main-activity classes that mean elaboration of food or drink for people — meat, prepared dishes, bakery and milling, preserves, dairy, nuts, fish, confectionery, drinks, condiments and the smaller classes — which return 3,221 rows for 1,745 distinct registrations. Handling, packing, bottling, storage, slaughter, cutting rooms, animal feed and non-food classes are excluded. De-duplicated by registration number, then against the area CSVs by name and municipality. 156 open Level 1 leads fall in this area.
- Cutoff: the dataset as queried on 2026-09-04. This closes the RIAAC source: the olive-oil, honey, cheese and wine classes were taken in the two earlier batches.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. RIAAC supports the registered establishment, its activity class and the industrial address it publishes, never current activity, a public own offer or online sales. Its `municipi` cell carries the locality after the municipality.
- Known weakness of this batch: only 335 of the 1,745 registrations matched a published row, against 46% for the wine batch. These classes carry far more industrial and contract manufacturing than the artisanal classes did, and a plant that produces only for third-party labels fails the own-offer criterion. Expect a high rejection rate on review; the activity class in each row is the discriminator to start from.

## RIAAC meat, bakery, preserve and other elaborators — identity reconciliation — 2026-09-04

- Discovery source: the [Indústries agroalimentàries de Catalunya](https://analisi.transparenciacatalunya.cat/Medi-Rural-Pesca/Ind-stries-agroaliment-ries-de-Catalunya/p28j-xe65) open dataset, which publishes the Registre d'Indústries Agràries i Alimentàries de Catalunya (RIAAC).
- Search date: 2026-09-04.
- Scope: the 69 remaining RIAAC main-activity classes that mean elaboration of food or drink for people — meat, prepared dishes, bakery and milling, preserves, dairy, nuts, fish, confectionery, drinks, condiments and the smaller classes — which return 3,221 rows for 1,745 distinct registrations. Handling, packing, bottling, storage, slaughter, cutting rooms, animal feed and non-food classes are excluded. De-duplicated by registration number, then against the area CSVs by name and municipality. 19 further registrations in this area sit in a municipality that has only one or two published rows of the same category, so each is held on one question: is it one of those rows under its trading name, or a different productive unit?
- Cutoff: the dataset as queried on 2026-09-04. This closes the RIAAC source: the olive-oil, honey, cheese and wine classes were taken in the two earlier batches.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. RIAAC supports the registered establishment, its activity class and the industrial address it publishes, never current activity, a public own offer or online sales. Its `municipi` cell carries the locality after the municipality.
- Known weakness of this batch: only 335 of the 1,745 registrations matched a published row, against 46% for the wine batch. These classes carry far more industrial and contract manufacturing than the artisanal classes did, and a plant that produces only for third-party labels fails the own-offer criterion. Expect a high rejection rate on review; the activity class in each row is the discriminator to start from.

## DO Costers del Segre & Lleida artisan wineries pass — 2026-09-04

- Discovery source: Official winery register of the Consell Regulador de la DO Costers del Segre (<https://www.costersdelsegre.es/ca/cellers>), official Ruta del Vi de Lleida directory, and artisan local wine festivals/directories (La BiVi de l'Albi, etc.).
- Search date: 2026-09-04.
- Scope: 7 open Level 1 wine producer leads in Lleida with confirmed productive activity and own offer, reconciled against `data/csv/es/catalunya/lleida.csv`, `data/evidence/es/catalunya/lleida.jsonl`, and existing candidate backlog. DeBresca in Baix Pallars is already published as `debresca-baix-pallars` (`Destilados y licores`, with wines L'Argenteria and La Geganta); Celler del Montsec is the wine section of the already cataloged `Cooperativa d'Artesa de Segre - ECO Artesa` (`cooperativa-dartesa-de-segre-artesa-de-segre`); Carviresa and Clos Tredòs are already queued in earlier passes.
- Cutoff: public directory listings and producer domains reviewed on 2026-09-04.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings. Denomination councils, certification registers, and route rosters support operator identity, product, and registered location. They do not by themselves prove current activity, an independent public own offer, or catalog admission.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## Venda de proximitat accredited producers — 2026-09-04

- Discovery source: the [Productors adherits a la venda de proximitat](https://analisi.transparenciacatalunya.cat/Medi-Rural-Pesca/Productors-adherits-a-la-venda-de-proximitat/xmyy-7xqi) open register, which accredits Catalan producers to sell their own output directly or through a short circuit.
- Search date: 2026-09-04.
- Scope: all 2,410 accredited producers, de-duplicated against the area CSVs on four keys — published phone, own email domain, name and municipality, industrial address and municipality — and against the leads already open in these notes. 1,284 resolved to something already held, which leaves 412 open Level 1 leads in this area.
- Cutoff: the register as published on 2026-09-04; its rows were last updated on 2026-07-03. Fourteen accreditations covering only ornamental plants or nursery stock were dropped as non-food, and nine fall outside the four Catalan provinces.
- Why this source is stronger than the registers above: accreditation is granted for selling the producer's **own** output directly, so unlike RIAAC or the RGSEAA it speaks to the own-offer criterion rather than only to a productive step. It still does not establish current activity, and the accreditation year on each row is the only currency signal the register gives.
- Source ceiling: see `data/csv/es/AGENTS.md` § Source ceilings.
